# A71 Checks Without the Owner (Plan 4D) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make the A71 checks of sub-projects 2 to 4 run without the owner, except for about 8 minutes of steps that only he can do (about 30 minutes near the computer), and fix the failures that his sitting of 2026-11-20 found: the emergency question on a scroll, a bare settings title that did nothing, and the One UI failures of Bluetooth, the ring volume, the text commands in Samsung Notes and the edge swipe.

**Architecture:** Tasks 1 to 5 change Kotlin. Task 1 fixes two rules of the instant commands (`EmergencyGuard`, `ScreenTargets`). Tasks 2 to 4 add debug hooks in `app/src/debug` that answer only counts, flags, numbers and hashes: a tag on each transcript that a script sends, with what that transcript's command did (H1), notice kinds (H6), a match of a played clip to its expected text on the phone (H2), two more marks of the phone's clock (H5), the microphone's level (H7), checks of the screen on the phone (H3) and three more fields of `state` (H4). Main code gets only small seams for them (a step watcher in `Orchestrator`, a transcript check in `Listening`, a route in `CommandResult`). Task 5 fixes the One UI failures and adds a clipboard hook that saves and puts back the owner's clipboard. Tasks 6 to 11 build the PowerShell side on these hooks, and Task 12 is the A71 run, which the controller starts with the owner; `docs\superpowers\plans\2026-11-20-a71-checks-4d-interfaces.md` fixes every name and answer between the two halves.

**Tech Stack:** Kotlin 2.4.21, kotlinx.coroutines 1.11.0, JUnit 4, AndroidX Test, the Android SDK's `AudioManager`, `ClipboardManager` and `NotificationManager`, `java.security.MessageDigest` (SHA-256), PowerShell 7.3 for `scripts\hook.ps1`, Pester 6.2.0 and PSScriptAnalyzer 1.25.0 for the scripts.

## Global Constraints

- Package and application ID `dev.kestrel`; licence GPL-3.0-or-later. No new dependency.
- `minSdk 33`, `targetSdk 37`, `compileSdk 37`. The A71 runs Android 13 (API 33) and One UI 5.1; every new Android call exists from API 33.
- The hooks exist only in the debug build (`app/src/debug`), behind `TestHookReceiver`, which only callers with `android.permission.DUMP` (in practice `adb shell`) reach. Release builds contain none of them, and the build-time audit checks that. Main code gets only the seams that this plan names; each one does nothing until a debug hook sets it.
- No answer of a hook holds what Kestrel heard, screen text, a label, a contact, a number from the screen or the clipboard's content. Answers hold counts, flags, numbers, bounds, kinds and hashes (the first 16 hexadecimal characters of SHA-256, `plan-4d-interfaces.md`, section 1). Nothing that this plan adds logs or prints screen text, the owner's words, account names or e-mail addresses, keys or tokens (spec 13.5); `toString` of anything that carries text prints only lengths.
- A check matches a result to the tag of the text that it sent, never to Kestrel's newest command.
- No hook sends "yes, call" on a device that is not an emulator (process rule 10); the scripts never dial and never answer "yes, call".
- Test only on `kestrel-api37` (check it with `adb -s <serial> emu avd name` in the same command as each device command); never on the S23+ or the AVD `zoran`. Tasks 1 to 11 never use the A71; Task 12, the A71 run, which the controller starts, is the only task that does. The controller holds the emulator lock (`.superpowers/sdd/progress.md`, process rule 9); never create, wait for or delete `.superpowers\sdd\emulator.lock`. Run only one Gradle build at a time on this computer (process rule 8b). Gradle only through `scripts\gradle.ps1`, stopped with `--stop` at the end of each task; device tests only through `scripts\connected-test.ps1` (with `-Pandroid.injected.androidTest.leaveApksInstalledAfterRun=true`); installs only through `scripts\install.ps1`.
- Scripts run on Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7, or declare `#Requires -Version 7.3` as `hook.ps1` does. `scripts\test-scripts.ps1` passes after every script change.
- The repository uses LF line endings.
- The plan file is named with the day it is written: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-20-a71-checks-4d.md`. The controller commits `.superpowers/sdd/plan-4d-interfaces.md` with it as `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-20-a71-checks-4d-interfaces.md`, which the code's comments and `hook.ps1`'s help name; this plan's text calls it `plan-4d-interfaces.md`. A plan is frozen once committed; a later difference goes into `.superpowers/sdd/progress.md`.

## Before you start

- The code is `origin/main` at `366d867`, or later commits of this plan. Plan 4C and its final fixes are complete. The counts at the start of this plan are those at `366d867`. The controller gives their values in each task's brief: `:app` JVM tests A₀ (with the debug-only JVM tests of `app/src/testDebug`, which `:app:testDebugUnitTest` runs too), `:core:voice` JVM V₀, `:core:screen` JVM S₀, the device tests of `:app` O₀, and Pester T₀ in each shell. Each task gives its counts as these values plus what it adds, as "N + k" where N is the count before the task; before each task, compare the counts with the ones that the earlier tasks left. If a count differs, stop and ask the controller.
- Before each task, check that every text that the task replaces exists, and exists once, in its file. If a text is missing, stop and ask the controller.
- Before each task, run `git status --short`. If a file that the task changes (its **Files** list) already shows changes that are not the task's own, stop and ask the controller, because the task's `git add` would commit them too.
- Run Gradle only through `scripts\gradle.ps1`, and stop it with `scripts\gradle.ps1 --stop` at the end of each task. Only one Gradle build may run on this computer at a time (process rule 8b); if another agent's build runs, wait for it to end.
- Tasks 2 to 5 and Tasks 7 to 12 (their dry runs and device steps on kestrel-api37) use the emulator. The controller holds the emulator's lock for each task that uses it (process rule 9) and says so in the task's brief; never create, wait for or delete `.superpowers\sdd\emulator.lock` yourself, and skip any step's "wait for the emulator lock and take it" and its "delete it". If the brief does not say that the controller holds the lock, ask the controller before the device steps. Every device command starts with a check of the emulator, in the same command: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; if ("$(& (Get-Adb) -s emulator-5554 emu avd name)" -notmatch '^kestrel-api37\b') { throw 'emulator-5554 is not kestrel-api37' };` and then the command itself. If the check throws, or no emulator runs, start the emulator with `scripts\start-emulator.ps1`, check that the serial that it prints belongs to `kestrel-api37`, and use that serial in place of `emulator-5554`. Never send a command to a connected phone.
- The device tests of `:app` need the speech models on the emulator (`scripts\push-models.ps1`, which earlier plans ran); a test that lacks them is skipped by `VoiceTests.assumeModels`, which the result file shows.
- Every commit message ends with the line `Co-authored-by: Copilot <223556219+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com>`.

## Not in this plan

- The fixtures that the map names for the A71 (a text field, a password field with "Show password", a MediaSession player): `:testapp` already has `CommandsActivity`, text fields, a WebView page and a media player, and writer B's tasks install it; this plan adds no debug activity.
- A reason code for failures in `voice-state` (inv-oneui-a71, 3): `said` and `shown` hashes of H1 give the failure's fixed sentence, which tells the failures apart without words.
- One UI's gesture style "Swipe from bottom" (overlay `com.samsung.internal.systemui.navbar.sec_gestural`): Task 5 treats it as gestures, as Android's own `navigation_mode` 2 does, until the owner checks it.
- Dropping a trailing "notification" after "expand", "collapse" and "dismiss" (inv-oneui-a71, 3, optional): it changes the grammar, which this plan does not touch.

## Changes to the design that this plan makes

- H4 (`state`'s new fields) moves from Task 2 to Task 4, because it reads the screen layer as H3 does; Task 2 keeps H1 and H6.
- `voice-state -Tag` answers `role`, `read` and `latest` besides the map's fields, and `said` and `shown` are lists of hashes, because a command may say several fixed sentences (an announcement, a question and its result).
- H2's strays are dropped while an expectation waits, so that a stray utterance never acts during an air check; `-NoRun` keeps dropping until its window ends.
- H5's two marks are plain times, `segmentClosed=<ms> transcribed=<ms>`, at the end of `voice-times`' timeline, so that the existing fields keep their order.
- H7 answers `rms` (all samples since the last read) and `frameRms` (the loudest 20 ms frame), because a calibration clip's silence would lower the first.
- `screen-has -Template '<text with {label}>' -Tag k` sends a transcript built from a label on the phone, so that the map's C21 ("long press <label>") never brings a label to the PC. The map's air variant of C21, which needs the label on the PC for `voice-synth`, is dropped.
- `voice-clip -Quiet` makes Kestrel hear only a silent stand-in microphone during the text pass (map, 7.1).
- For sub-project 5 (.superpowers/sdd/plan-5-design.md, "Shared files and anchors", items 1, 2 and 4), Task 2 also stores the number of the task that a tagged transcript starts (`voice-state -Tag` answers `role=task` and `task=<n>`), passes the transcript's extras `model` and `live` to the debug-only store `TaskOptions` before the task runs, with the seam `Listening.taskOptions` from which sub-project 5's `AgentRunner` will read them (nothing reads it in this plan), and tests that the clip microphone delivers silence with nothing queued (`voice-clip -Quiet`). The anchors `// sub-project 5B` are in `VoiceHooks.taskOptions` (H1's extras) and in `ScreenQuery`'s filter check (H3's kinds of match).
- The `clipboard` hook (Task 5) saves the clipboard while Kestrel's settings are in front, where Android lets Kestrel read it, keeps the copy only in Kestrel's memory, and puts it back after a check that copies; this answers the controller's rule that no check leaves test text on the clipboard where it can avoid it. A read that Android refuses answers `clip=unknown` and saves nothing, a restore never empties the clipboard, and a copy that Android refuses to put back is reported and forgotten. Its device test runs only on an emulator, in a package that no A71 script runs, and puts back the clip that the device had.

## Threads

| Call | Thread | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Orchestrator`'s `onStep` and `Listening.steps` | the Orchestrator's coroutine (`Listening`'s controller thread) | runs after each step's state is set and before its effects; must not throw (a failure goes to `onError`) |
| `CommandTags.onStep`, `register`, `tagged`, `record` | any | `@Synchronized`; the hooks read on `AppGraph.scope` |
| `Listening.transcripts` and `ExpectHooks`' check | the voice session's thread that calls `Listener.send` | must return quickly: a word comparison and a parse, no binder call |
| `LevelMeter.add` | the microphone's thread | `report()` from the hook's coroutine; both `@Synchronized` |
| `ScreenHooks` | `AppGraph.scope`; the overlays' state on `Dispatchers.Main` | reads `snapshots.current()` as `snapshot` does |
| `SessionHooks`' clipboard | `Dispatchers.Main` for `ClipboardManager` | the saved clip stays in the process's memory only |
| `DeviceCommands`, `TextCommands`, `ScreenCommands` | the command's job in `AppGraph.scope` | as before |

### Task 1: The emergency guard and a row's title

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/commands/CallCommands.kt` (`EmergencyGuard.Reach.Point`, `needsYes`, `reaches`)
- Modify: `app/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/commands/ScreenTargets.kt` (`label`, new `matchNames`, `title` and `inside`)
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-kestrel-design.md` (8.7, 9.6)
- Test: `app/src/test/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/commands/PhoneCallsTest.kt`, `app/src/test/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/commands/ScreenTargetsTest.kt`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `EmergencyGuard.needsYes(reaches, view, phoneApps, emergency, unreadable)`, `EmergencyGuard.reaches(command, screen)`, `ScreenTargets.decide(view, target, kind, strictness, cells)`, `ScreenTargets.names(snapshot, element)` as they are at `366d867`.
- Produces: `EmergencyGuard.Reach.Point(x: Int, y: Int, scroll: Boolean = false)`. When a spoken label is matched (`ScreenTargets.decide` with a `Target.Label`, and so also the emergency guard's and `checkIn`'s decisions), an actionable element that has no text, description, hint or supplemental description of its own also has its title as a name: the text of the first element inside it that takes no touch. `ScreenTargets.names` itself is unchanged, so its other callers (the call button's search, the Done button, the password toggle, the search fields, the elements named with a number, "What is N?" and `DeviceCommands.namesRadio`) see the same names as before.

inv-commands-a71 found two root causes. First, the guard checks "scroll to top" as a tap at the screen's centre, and R1 counts any element named "emergency" or "SOS" under that point, so on Settings' main page the row "Safety and emergency" made Kestrel ask "That may call an emergency number…". A scroll that Kestrel aims itself presses no element (it uses the list's scroll action or a swipe across the list, or across the window when no list scrolls, longer than the touch slop), so R1 must not count for it; R2 (the phone apps' windows) still does. Swipes, pinches and drags that the owner names stay as they are. Second, a settings row is named after all the texts inside it ("Display Brightness • Eye comfort shield…"), so a bare "Display" only starts the name, scores 0.85 and stays below the bare limit of 0.90. The row's title becomes one more name; its summary does not, and the strict limits stay.

```kotlin
    @Test
    fun aScrollThatKestrelAimsItselfPressesNoElementSoANamedRowUnderTheCentreNeedsNoYes() {
        val settings = "com.android.settings"
        val window = WindowSummary(1, WindowKind.APPLICATION, packageName = settings, bounds = screen)
        val snapshot = ScreenSnapshot(
            0,
            listOf(window),
            listOf(
                element("list", 1, screen, settings, description = "Settings list", clickable = false).copy(scrollable = true),
                element("display", 1, IntRect(0, 900, 1080, 1100), settings, text = "Display Brightness", parent = 0),
                // As on Settings' main page after "scroll down": the row covers the screen's centre, (540, 1200).
                element("safety", 1, IntRect(0, 1100, 1080, 1300), settings, text = "Safety & emergency Emergency SOS, medical info, alerts", parent = 0),
            ),
        )
        val view = ScreenView(snapshot, emptyList(), grid = null)
        fun yes(command: Command) =
            EmergencyGuard.needsYes(EmergencyGuard.reaches(command, screen), view, EmergencyGuard.PHONE_PACKAGES, noEmergency)

        assertTrue(EmergencyGuard.emergencyNamed(snapshot, 2))
        for (command in listOf(
            Command.ScrollToEnd(top = true, target = null), Command.ScrollToEnd(top = false, target = null),
            Command.Scroll(Direction.DOWN, null), Command.SwipePage(forward = true),
            Command.Scroll(Direction.DOWN, Target.Label("settings list")),
        )) {
            assertFalse(command.toString(), yes(command))
        }
        // R1 still guards a tap on the row, and a pinch that the owner names at the centre.
        assertTrue(yes(Command.Tap(Target.Label("safety & emergency"))))
        assertTrue(yes(Command.Pinch(out = true, target = null)))
    }

    @Test
```
```kotlin
    fun aBareTitleTapsARowThatIsNamedAfterTheTextsInsideItAndItsSummaryIsNoName() {
        fun row(key: String, title: String, summary: String, top: Int, index: Int) = listOf(
            element(key, "$title $summary", IntRect(0, top, 1080, top + 200), className = "android.widget.LinearLayout"),
            element("$key-title", title, IntRect(40, top + 20, 600, top + 100), clickable = false, parent = index, className = "android.widget.TextView")
                .copy(text = title),
            element("$key-summary", summary, IntRect(40, top + 100, 1000, top + 180), clickable = false, parent = index, className = "android.widget.TextView")
                .copy(text = summary),
        )
        // One UI's main page of Settings: each row is named after its title and its summary.
        val rows = row("notifications", "Notifications", "App notifications • Do not disturb", 300, 0) +
            row("display", "Display", "Brightness • Eye comfort shield • Navigation bar", 500, 3) +
            row("wallpaper", "Wallpaper and style", "Wallpapers • Colour palette", 700, 6)
        val settings = ScreenView(ScreenSnapshot(0, listOf(app), rows), badges = emptyList(), grid = null)
        fun bare(spoken: String) = ScreenTargets.decide(settings, Target.Label(spoken), TargetKind.BARE, Strictness.STRICT, cells = false)

        assertEquals(Resolution.One(ScreenTargets.chosen(rows[3])), bare("Display."))
        // Only the match of a spoken label gets the title; the other users of names() see the row's names as before.
        assertEquals(listOf("Display Brightness • Eye comfort shield • Navigation bar"), ScreenTargets.names(settings.snapshot, rows[3]))
        // The summary is no name of its own, and a longer start of the name stays below the strict limits.
        assertFalse(bare("Brightness") is Resolution.One)
        assertFalse(bare("Display Brightness") is Resolution.One)
        // An element with a name of its own keeps only its own names.
        assertEquals(listOf("Send"), ScreenTargets.names(snapshot, snapshot.element("send")!!))
    }
```

In the same file, replace:

```kotlin
        private fun ownNames(element: ScreenElement): List<String> = listOfNotNull(
```

with:

```kotlin
        // The title of an actionable element that has no text, description, hint or supplemental description of its own, and
        // so is named after the texts inside it: the text of the first element inside it that takes no touch. The snapshot
        // lists the elements inside an element right after it. A summary or a later text is no name of its own, so that a
        // bare "On" never taps the one row whose summary says it.
        private fun title(snapshot: ScreenSnapshot, element: ScreenElement): String? {
            if (!element.isActionable || element.text != null || element.contentDescription != null || element.hint != null ||
                element.supplementalDescription != null
            ) {
                return null
            }
            val index = snapshot.elements.indexOfFirst { it.key == element.key }
            if (index < 0) return null
            for (inner in index + 1 until snapshot.elements.size) {
                if (!inside(snapshot, inner, index)) return null
                val candidate = snapshot.elements[inner]
                if (!candidate.isActionable) candidate.text?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { return it }
            }
            return null
        }

        // Whether the element at [index] lies inside the element at [ancestor], at most MAX_DEPTH levels down.
        private fun inside(snapshot: ScreenSnapshot, index: Int, ancestor: Int): Boolean {
            var parent = snapshot.elements[index].parent
            var depth = 0
            while (parent != null && depth <= MAX_DEPTH) {
                if (parent == ancestor) return true
                parent = snapshot.elements.getOrNull(parent)?.parent
                depth++
            }
            return false
        }

        // The names that a spoken label is matched with: the names of [element], and, for a row that is named after the texts
        // inside it, its title, so that a bare title taps a settings row (plan 4D; inv-commands-a71). Only the match of a
        // spoken label uses it; every other user of names() sees the names as before.
        private fun matchNames(snapshot: ScreenSnapshot, element: ScreenElement): List<String> =
            (names(snapshot, element) + listOfNotNull(title(snapshot, element))).distinct()

        private fun ownNames(element: ScreenElement): List<String> = listOfNotNull(
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Rules`, `State`, `Step`, `Event`, `Effect`, `Reply`, `Feedback`, `Work` (package `dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator`); `Listening.recentCommand(number)`, `Listening.state`, `Listening.microphones`; `EmergencyGuard.confirmed(answer)`; `Requests.read(transcript, spotted)`; `ProblemNotifications.STOPPED_ID`, `ACCESSIBILITY_ID`, `SETTINGS_ID`; `StopReason.BATTERY_RESTRICTED.text`; `ListeningTexts.CANCELLED`.
- Produces: `Orchestrator(..., onStep: (Event, State, Step) -> Unit = { _, _, _ -> })`; `@Volatile internal var Listening.steps: ((Event, State, Step) -> Unit)?`; `@Volatile internal var Listening.taskOptions: ((task: Int) -> Map<String, String>)?` (the seam that sub-project 5's `AgentRunner` reads, once per task, for `model` and `live`; null, and so no options, outside the debug hooks; nothing reads it in this plan); `CommandResult.detail: String? = null` (last parameter); `DeviceCommands.ROUTE_PANEL = "panel"`, `ROUTE_TILE = "tile"`, `ROUTE_PAGE = "page"` and `bluetoothResult(changed: Boolean, route: String)`; `internal class CommandTags` with `enum class Role { PENDING, COMMAND, TASK, ANSWER, CANCEL, NONE }`, `data class Tagged(role, command, read, task = 0)`, `data class Record(said: List<String>, shown: List<String>, detail: String?)`, `data class StartedTask(task: Int, options: Map<String, String>)`, `register(tag, event, read, options = emptyMap()): Boolean`, `tagged(tag): Tagged?`, `record(command): Record`, `onStep(event, before, step): StartedTask?` and `companion fun hash(text: String): String`; `internal class TaskOptions` (debug only) with `put(task, options)` and `take(task): Map<String, String>`; in `VoiceHooks`' companion: `val tags: CommandTags`, `val tasks: TaskOptions`, `fun tag(graph: AppGraph, tag: String, event: Event.Heard, options: Map<String, String> = emptyMap())`, `fun taskOptions(model: String?, live: Boolean?): Map<String, String>` (with the anchor `// sub-project 5B`), `fun readOf(text: String, named: Boolean): String`, `fun refused(text: String, hardware: String): Boolean`, `fun kindAndOutcome(graph: AppGraph, number: Int): Pair<String, String>`, `const val YES_CALL_REFUSED`; `SessionHooks.noticeKinds(notices: List<Pair<Int, String?>>): String` and the command `CHECK_NOTICE`; `hook.ps1`'s `-Tag`, `-Quiet`, `-Live` and `check-notice` (its existing `-Model` gives `--es model`). Task 3 tags a matched utterance with `VoiceHooks.tag` and answers with `VoiceHooks.kindAndOutcome`; Task 4's `screen-has -Template` sends with `VoiceHooks.tag`. The answers are those of `plan-4d-interfaces.md`, sections 2 and 3.

Hook H1 of the automation map. Today a check of the A71 scripts reads "Kestrel's newest command", so a stray utterance or music before the check's own command took its place (19 checks of the sitting on 2026-11-20). From now on a script sends each command as text with a tag, `voice -Send heard -Text t -Tag k`, and reads what exactly that transcript did with `voice-state -Tag k`. The Orchestrator gets one seam, a watcher of its steps that sees each event with the state before it and the step that it caused, on the Orchestrator's own coroutine and before the step's effects run; `CommandTags` (debug only) follows the tagged events by identity, so that no other transcript can take a tag, and records for each command the hashes of what Kestrel said and showed and the route that a radio took (`CommandResult.detail`). For sub-project 5 (its design's "Shared files and anchors", items 1, 2 and 4): a tagged transcript that starts a task ("Kestrel, open the maps.") gets the task's number, which `voice-state -Tag k` answers as `task=`, so that sub-project 5's `agent-task -Result -Tag k` finds it; the transcript's extras `--es model` and `--ez live` go to the debug-only store `TaskOptions` under that task number before the task runs, and `Listening.taskOptions` is the seam through which sub-project 5's `AgentRunner` will read them (null in release builds, where no hook sets it); and `voice-clip -Quiet` makes the clip microphone, with nothing queued, deliver silence as Kestrel's microphone. On a phone, the hook refuses to send "yes, call" (process rule 10). `voice-clip -Quiet` gives Kestrel a silent stand-in microphone during the text pass, so that the room does not start commands at all. H6: `notices` names the kinds of Kestrel's notices in the shade, so that the battery-rules step needs no look at the shade, and `check-notice` posts and takes away Kestrel's own notification "Kestrel check", which the notification checks expand, collapse and dismiss.

Create `app/src/testDebug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/CommandTagsTest.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.app.debug

import dev.kestrel.app.listen.ListeningTexts
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.Event
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.Rules
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.State
import dev.kestrel.commands.CommandResult
import dev.kestrel.commands.Outcome
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

/** The tags of plan 4D's checks (hook H1), on the Orchestrator's real rules. */
class CommandTagsTest {
    private val tags = CommandTags()
    private var state = State()

    // Applies the rules to [event] as the Orchestrator does, and gives the step to the tags.
    private fun step(event: Event) {
        val before = state
        val step = Rules.next(before, event)
        state = step.state
        tags.onStep(event, before, step)
    }

    private fun send(text: String, tag: String? = null) {
        val event = Event.Heard(text, named = false)
        if (tag != null) assertTrue(tags.register(tag, event, VoiceHooks.readOf(text, named = false)))
        step(event)
    }

    @Test
    fun aTaggedTranscriptGetsItsOwnCommandItsAnswerAndWhatTheCommandSaidShowedAndTook() {
        step(Event.Toggle)
        val pending = Event.Heard("Tap Delete.", named = false)
        assertTrue(tags.register("ask", pending, "other"))
        assertEquals(CommandTags.Tagged(CommandTags.Role.PENDING, 0, "other"), tags.tagged("ask"))

        step(pending)
        // A stray transcript while the command runs takes nothing from the tag.
        send("Scroll down.")
        step(Event.CommandAsks(1, "Which one?"))
        send("1", tag = "answer")
        step(Event.CommandEnded(1, CommandResult(Outcome.DONE, say = "Done.", detail = "tile")))

        assertEquals(CommandTags.Tagged(CommandTags.Role.COMMAND, 1, "other"), tags.tagged("ask"))
        assertEquals(CommandTags.Role.ANSWER, tags.tagged("answer")?.role)
        assertEquals(1, tags.tagged("answer")?.command)
        val record = tags.record(1)
        assertEquals(listOf(CommandTags.hash("Which one?"), CommandTags.hash("Done.")), record.said)
        assertEquals(listOf(CommandTags.hash("Which one?")), record.shown)
        assertEquals("tile", record.detail)
        assertEquals(CommandTags.Record(), tags.record(2))
    }

    @Test
    fun aStopCancelsItsOwnCommandAndAnAnswerThatIsNoAnswerBecomesTheCommandThatItStarts() {
        step(Event.Toggle)
        send("Scroll down.", tag = "scroll")
        send("Stop.", tag = "stop")

        assertEquals(CommandTags.Tagged(CommandTags.Role.CANCEL, 1, "stop"), tags.tagged("stop"))
        assertEquals(listOf(CommandTags.hash(ListeningTexts.CANCELLED)), tags.record(1).shown)

        send("Tap Send.", tag = "send")
        step(Event.CommandAsks(2, "Which one?"))
        send("Scroll up.", tag = "other")
        assertEquals(CommandTags.Role.ANSWER, tags.tagged("other")?.role)
        step(Event.CommandEnded(2, CommandResult(Outcome.CANCELLED, passOn = "Scroll up.")))

        assertEquals(CommandTags.Tagged(CommandTags.Role.COMMAND, 3, "other"), tags.tagged("other"))

        // A later untagged answer that is passed on leaves the earlier tagged answer as it was.
        step(Event.CommandEnded(3, CommandResult(Outcome.DONE)))
        send("Tap Send.", tag = "pick")
        step(Event.CommandAsks(4, "Which one?"))
        send("1", tag = "one")
        step(Event.CommandAsks(4, "Which one?"))
        send("Scroll down.")
        step(Event.CommandEnded(4, CommandResult(Outcome.CANCELLED, passOn = "Scroll down.")))
        assertEquals(CommandTags.Role.ANSWER, tags.tagged("one")?.role)
        assertEquals(4, tags.tagged("one")?.command)
    }

    @Test
    fun aTagIsUsedOnceATranscriptWhileKestrelIsOffStartsNothingAndTheHashIsSha256() {
        send("Scroll down.", tag = "off")

        assertEquals(CommandTags.Tagged(CommandTags.Role.NONE, 0, "other"), tags.tagged("off"))
        assertFalse(tags.register("off", Event.Heard("Scroll up.", named = false), "other"))
        assertNull(tags.tagged("never"))
        // A transcript that a hook drops before the Orchestrator sees it did nothing.
        val dropped = Event.Heard("Scroll up.", named = false)
        assertTrue(tags.register("dropped", dropped, "other"))
        tags.dropped(dropped)
        assertEquals(CommandTags.Tagged(CommandTags.Role.NONE, 0, "other"), tags.tagged("dropped"))
        // The first 16 hexadecimal characters of SHA-256("abc"), as the plan's interfaces' Get-TextHash (2026-11-20-a71-checks-4d-interfaces.md) gives them.
        assertEquals("ba7816bf8f01cfea", CommandTags.hash("abc"))
    }

    @Test
    fun aTaggedTranscriptThatStartsATaskGetsTheTasksNumberAndPassesItsOptionsBeforeTheTaskRuns() {
        step(Event.Toggle)
        val task = Event.Heard("Kestrel, open the maps.", named = true)
        assertTrue(tags.register("task", task, VoiceHooks.readOf(task.text, named = true), mapOf("model" to "test-model")))

        val before = state
        val step = Rules.next(before, task)
        state = step.state
        val started = tags.onStep(task, before, step)

        assertEquals(CommandTags.StartedTask(1, mapOf("model" to "test-model")), started)
        assertEquals(CommandTags.Tagged(CommandTags.Role.TASK, 0, "task", task = 1), tags.tagged("task"))
        // An untagged task passes nothing.
        step(Event.TaskEnded(1, reply = null))
        val other = Event.Heard("Kestrel, open the settings.", named = true)
        val next = Rules.next(state, other)
        assertNull(tags.onStep(other, state, next))
    }
}
```

Create `app/src/testDebug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/HookRulesTest.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.app.debug

import dev.kestrel.app.listen.ProblemNotifications
import dev.kestrel.app.listen.StopReason
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

/** The rules of plan 4D's hooks that need no device. */
class HookRulesTest {
    @Test
    fun yesCallIsRefusedOnAPhoneAndSentOnAnEmulator() {
        for (text in listOf("Yes, call.", "yes call", "Yes, call, Kestrel.")) {
            assertTrue(text, VoiceHooks.refused(text, "qcom"))
            assertFalse(text, VoiceHooks.refused(text, "ranchu"))
            assertFalse(text, VoiceHooks.refused(text, "goldfish"))
        }
        assertFalse(VoiceHooks.refused("Yes.", "qcom"))
        assertFalse(VoiceHooks.refused("Call 1234.", "qcom"))
    }

    @Test
    fun theNoticesKindsComeFromTheirIdsAndTheStopNoticesReason() {
        assertEquals("none", SessionHooks.noticeKinds(emptyList()))
        assertEquals(
            "accessibility,battery",
            SessionHooks.noticeKinds(
                listOf(ProblemNotifications.STOPPED_ID to StopReason.BATTERY_RESTRICTED.text, ProblemNotifications.ACCESSIBILITY_ID to "x"),
            ),
        )
        assertEquals(
            "settings,stopped",
            SessionHooks.noticeKinds(listOf(ProblemNotifications.SETTINGS_ID to null, ProblemNotifications.STOPPED_ID to "Other")),
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun aTasksOptionsAreTheGivenExtrasAndAreReadOnce() {
        assertEquals(emptyMap<String, String>(), VoiceHooks.taskOptions(model = null, live = null))
        assertEquals(mapOf("model" to "test-model", "live" to "false"), VoiceHooks.taskOptions(model = "test-model", live = false))

        val store = TaskOptions()
        store.put(3, mapOf("live" to "true"))
        assertEquals(mapOf("live" to "true"), store.take(3))
        assertEquals(emptyMap<String, String>(), store.take(3))
        // It keeps only the newest tasks' options.
        for (task in 1..TaskOptions.MAX_TASKS + 1) store.put(task, mapOf("model" to "m$task"))
        assertEquals(emptyMap<String, String>(), store.take(1))
        assertEquals(mapOf("model" to "m2"), store.take(2))
    }
}
```

```kotlin
    val say: String? = null,
    val show: String? = null,
    val repeatable: Boolean = false,
    val passOn: String? = null,
    val acted: Command? = null,
) {

}
```

```kotlin
 * Orchestrator reads as a new utterance. [acted]: the command that acted, when it differs from the one that ran, as

 * or "page" for the radios, which the debug hook voice-state reports (plan 4D); never text of the screen or the owner.
 */
data class CommandResult(

    val say: String? = null,
    val show: String? = null,
    val repeatable: Boolean = false,
    val passOn: String? = null,
    val acted: Command? = null,
    val detail: String? = null,
) {

        "CommandResult($outcome, say=${say?.length}, show=${show?.length}, repeatable=$repeatable, " +
            "passOn=${passOn?.length}, acted=${acted?.javaClass?.simpleName}, detail=$detail)"
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `CommandTags`**

Create `app/src/debug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/CommandTags.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.app.debug

import dev.kestrel.app.listen.ListeningTexts
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.Effect
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.Event
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.Feedback
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.Reply
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.State
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.Step
import dev.kestrel.app.orchestrator.Work
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.util.IdentityHashMap

/**
 * The tags of the transcripts that the automatic A71 checks send (plan 4D, hook H1): which command each tagged transcript
 * started, answered or cancelled, or which task it started, and for each command the hashes of what Kestrel said and
 * showed and the route that it took. [onStep] reads every step of the Orchestrator on the Orchestrator's coroutine, and
 * finds a tagged transcript by its identity, so that no other transcript with the same words takes its tag; the hooks read
 * on theirs. A tagged transcript's options (the hook's extras, such as `model` and `live`) come back from [onStep] with
 * the number of the task that it started, before the task runs, for sub-project 5's AgentRunner. It keeps the newest
 * [MAX_TAGS] tags and [MAX_COMMANDS] commands, in memory only, and holds tags, numbers, kinds, options and hashes, never
 * words.
 */
internal class CommandTags {
    enum class Role { PENDING, COMMAND, TASK, ANSWER, CANCEL, NONE }

    /**
     * What a tagged transcript did: its [role] for [command] (0 for none), how Kestrel's Requests read it, and the number
     * of the [task] that it started (0 for none).
     */
    data class Tagged(val role: Role, val command: Int, val read: String, val task: Int = 0)

    /** What a command said and showed, as hashes, oldest first, at most [MAX_TEXTS] each, and its route. */
    data class Record(val said: List<String> = emptyList(), val shown: List<String> = emptyList(), val detail: String? = null)

    /** Task number [task] started from a tagged transcript that carried [options]. */
    data class StartedTask(val task: Int, val options: Map<String, String>)

    private val tags = LinkedHashMap<String, Tagged>()
    private val waiting = IdentityHashMap<Event.Heard, String>()
    private val options = HashMap<String, Map<String, String>>()
    private val answered = HashMap<Int, String>()
    private val records = LinkedHashMap<Int, Record>()

    /**
     * Links [tag] and the hook's [options] to [event] before Kestrel handles it; false when the tag is among the newest
     * ones already.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun register(tag: String, event: Event.Heard, read: String, options: Map<String, String> = emptyMap()): Boolean {
        if (tag in tags) return false
        tags[tag] = Tagged(Role.PENDING, 0, read)
        waiting[event] = tag
        if (options.isNotEmpty()) this.options[tag] = options
        while (tags.size > MAX_TAGS) {
            val oldest = tags.keys.first()
            tags.remove(oldest)
            waiting.values.remove(oldest)
            this.options.remove(oldest)
        }
        return true
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun tagged(tag: String): Tagged? = tags[tag]

    /** [event] never reaches the Orchestrator, as a match that a hook drops: its tag, if it has one, did nothing. */
    @Synchronized
    fun dropped(event: Event.Heard) {
        val tag = waiting.remove(event) ?: return
        options.remove(tag)
        tags[tag]?.let { tags[tag] = it.copy(role = Role.NONE, command = 0) }
    }

    @Synchronized
    fun record(command: Int): Record = records[command] ?: Record()

    /**
     * Notes what the Orchestrator's [step] after [event], from the state [before], did for the tags and the commands. When
     * a tagged transcript started a task, returns its number with the transcript's options; null otherwise.
     */
    @Synchronized
    fun onStep(event: Event, before: State, step: Step): StartedTask? {
        val running = if (before.work == Work.RUNNING_COMMAND) before.command else 0
        val started = step.effects.filterIsInstance<Effect.RunCommand>().lastOrNull()
        val task = step.effects.filterIsInstance<Effect.RunTask>().lastOrNull()
        val tag = (event as? Event.Heard)?.let { waiting.remove(it) }
        val tagged = tag?.let { tags[it] }
        val answer = step.effects.filterIsInstance<Effect.AnswerCommand>().firstOrNull()
        var startedTask: StartedTask? = null
        if (tag != null && tagged != null) {
            tags[tag] = when {
                started != null -> tagged.copy(role = Role.COMMAND, command = started.command)
                task != null -> tagged.copy(role = Role.TASK, command = 0, task = task.task)
                answer != null -> tagged.copy(role = Role.ANSWER, command = answer.command)
                running > 0 && Effect.CancelCommand in step.effects -> tagged.copy(role = Role.CANCEL, command = running)
                else -> tagged.copy(role = Role.NONE, command = 0)
            }
            val given = options.remove(tag).orEmpty()
            if (task != null && started == null) startedTask = StartedTask(task.task, given)
        }
        // The newest answer to each command: its tag, or none when it was not tagged, so that an answer that is passed on
        // moves only its own tag.
        if (answer != null && answer.text != null) {
            if (tag != null && tags[tag]?.role == Role.ANSWER) answered[answer.command] = tag else answered.remove(answer.command)
        }
        // A cancelled command ends without CommandEnded, so its entry goes here.
        if (running > 0 && Effect.CancelCommand in step.effects) answered.remove(running)
        if (event is Event.CommandEnded) {
            event.result.detail?.let { detail -> update(event.command) { it.copy(detail = detail) } }
            // An answer that was no answer: the Orchestrator read it as a new utterance, which started a command.
            val passed = answered.remove(event.command)
            if (event.result.passOn != null && started != null && passed != null) {
                tags[passed]?.let { tags[passed] = it.copy(role = Role.COMMAND, command = started.command) }
            }
        }
        var about = when (event) {
            is Event.CommandEnded -> event.command
            is Event.CommandSays -> event.command
            is Event.CommandAsks -> event.command
            is Event.CommandCounts -> event.command
            else -> running
        }
        for (effect in step.effects) {
            when {
                // What comes after a command's start in the same step is that command's.
                effect is Effect.RunCommand -> about = effect.command
                effect is Effect.Say -> when (val reply = effect.reply) {
                    is Reply.Command -> add(reply.command, said = reply.text)
                    is Reply.Text -> if (event is Event.CommandEnded) add(about, said = reply.text)
                    else -> Unit
                }
                effect is Effect.Show -> when (val feedback = effect.feedback) {
                    is Feedback.CommandText -> add(about, shown = feedback.text)
                    Feedback.CommandCancelled -> add(about, shown = ListeningTexts.CANCELLED)
                    else -> Unit
                }
            }
        }
        return startedTask
    }

    private fun add(command: Int, said: String? = null, shown: String? = null) {
        if (command <= 0) return
        update(command) { record ->
            record.copy(
                said = said?.let { (record.said + hash(it)).takeLast(MAX_TEXTS) } ?: record.said,
                shown = shown?.let { (record.shown + hash(it)).takeLast(MAX_TEXTS) } ?: record.shown,
            )
        }
    }

    private fun update(command: Int, change: (Record) -> Record) {
        records[command] = change(records[command] ?: Record())
        while (records.size > MAX_COMMANDS) records.remove(records.keys.first())
    }

    companion object {
        const val MAX_TAGS = 64
        const val MAX_COMMANDS = 32
        const val MAX_TEXTS = 8

        /** The first 16 hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 of [text]'s UTF-8 bytes (2026-11-20-a71-checks-4d-interfaces.md, section 1). */
        fun hash(text: String): String =
            MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256").digest(text.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)).take(8)
                .joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it.toInt() and 0xff) }
    }
}
```

Create `app/src/debug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/TaskOptions.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.app.debug

/**
 * The options that a tagged transcript carried, by the number of the task that it started (plan 4D, for sub-project 5):
 * the hook's extras `model` and `live`, which sub-project 5's AgentRunner reads once per task through
 * `Listening.taskOptions`. Debug only: release builds have no hook that sets that seam, so a task there has no options.
 * It keeps the newest [MAX_TASKS] tasks' options, in memory only. Any thread may call it.
 */
internal class TaskOptions {
    private val byTask = LinkedHashMap<Int, Map<String, String>>()

    @Synchronized
    fun put(task: Int, options: Map<String, String>) {
        byTask[task] = options
        while (byTask.size > MAX_TASKS) byTask.remove(byTask.keys.first())
    }

    /** The options of task number [task], which this read removes; empty when it has none. */
    @Synchronized
    fun take(task: Int): Map<String, String> = byTask.remove(task).orEmpty()

    companion object {
        const val MAX_TASKS = 16
    }
}
```

```kotlin
        val given = intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_TAG)
        if (given != null) {
            if (event !is Event.Heard) throw HookError("only a transcript takes a tag; add --es event heard")
            val live = if (intent.hasExtra(EXTRA_LIVE)) intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_LIVE, false) else null
            tag(graph, given, event, taskOptions(intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_MODEL), live))
        }
        graph.listening.send(event)
        return if (given != null) "sent $name tag=$given" else "sent $name"
    }

    // Plan 4D: "yes, call" reaches Kestrel only on an emulator, never on a phone (process rule 10).
    private fun heard(intent: Intent): Event.Heard {
        val text = text(intent)
        if (refused(text, Build.HARDWARE)) throw HookError(YES_CALL_REFUSED)
        return Event.Heard(text, intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_NAMED, false))
    }

    // What the transcript with [tag] did (plan 4D, hook H1; 2026-11-20-a71-checks-4d-interfaces.md, section 2): never words.
    private fun tagState(tag: String): String {
        val tagged = tags.tagged(tag) ?: throw HookError("no transcript has the tag $tag")
        val state = graph.listening.state.value
        val number = tagged.command
        val (kind, outcome) = kindAndOutcome(graph, number)
        val runningNow = number > 0 && number == state.command && state.work == Work.RUNNING_COMMAND
        val taskNow = tagged.task > 0 && tagged.task == state.task && state.work == Work.RUNNING_TASK
        val phase = when {
            tagged.role == CommandTags.Role.PENDING -> "pending"
            tagged.task > 0 -> if (taskNow) "running" else "ended"
            number == 0 -> "none"
            runningNow && state.counting -> "countdown"
            runningNow && state.asking -> "question"
            runningNow -> "running"
            else -> "ended"
        }
        val record = tags.record(number)
        return "tag=$tag role=${tagged.role.name.lowercase()} command=$number task=${tagged.task} kind=$kind outcome=$outcome " +
            "asking=${runningNow && state.asking} phase=$phase detail=${record.detail ?: "none"} said=${hashes(record.said)} " +
            "shown=${hashes(record.shown)} read=${tagged.read} latest=${state.command}"
    }

    private fun hashes(list: List<String>): String = list.joinToString(",").ifEmpty { "none" }

    // Plan 4D: the silent clip microphone, so that no sound of the room reaches Kestrel during the checks' text pass.
    private fun quiet(): String {
        microphone.clear()
        val recording = synchronized(lock) {
            val listening = graph.listening
            if (listening.microphones !== clipFactory) {
                replaced = listening.microphones
                listening.microphones = clipFactory
            }
            microphone.recording
        }
        return if (recording) {
            "quiet: Kestrel hears only the silent clip microphone, until voice-clip -Clear or the end of its process"
        } else {
            "quiet: Kestrel hears only the silent clip microphone from its next start, until voice-clip -Clear or the end of its process"
        }
    }
```

```kotlin
        /** The microphone that VOICE_CLIP says its texts into, one for the whole process. */

```

with:

```kotlin
        /** The microphone that VOICE_CLIP says its texts into, one for the whole process. */
        val microphone: ClipMicrophone by lazy { ClipMicrophone() }

        /** The tags of plan 4D's checks, one record for the whole process. */
        val tags: CommandTags by lazy { CommandTags() }

        /** The options of the tasks that tagged transcripts started, for sub-project 5's AgentRunner (Listening.taskOptions). */
        val tasks: TaskOptions by lazy { TaskOptions() }

        const val YES_CALL_REFUSED = "only the emulator may answer \"yes, call\""

        private val TAG = Regex("[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,32}")

        /** Whether [text] is "yes, call" on a device whose [hardware] is not an emulator's, which the hooks never send. */
        fun refused(text: String, hardware: String): Boolean =
            hardware != "ranchu" && hardware != "goldfish" && EmergencyGuard.confirmed(text)

        /**
         * Links [tag] and [options] to [event] before Kestrel handles it, and starts the record of the Orchestrator's steps
         * (plan 4D, H1): a task that a tagged transcript starts gets its options in [tasks] before it runs, and
         * Listening.taskOptions reads them from there.
         */
        fun tag(graph: AppGraph, tag: String, event: Event.Heard, options: Map<String, String> = emptyMap()) {
            if (!TAG.matches(tag)) throw HookError("add --es tag with 1 to 32 letters, digits, '-' and '_'")
            val listening = graph.listening
            if (listening.steps == null) {
                listening.steps = { heard, before, step -> tags.onStep(heard, before, step)?.let { tasks.put(it.task, it.options) } }
                listening.taskOptions = tasks::take
            }
            if (!tags.register(tag, event, readOf(event.text, event.named), options)) {
                throw HookError("the tag $tag is in use; give each transcript a new tag")
            }
        }

        /**
         * The options that a tagged transcript passes to the task that it starts, from the hook's extras: --es model and
         * --ez live, which sub-project 5's AgentRunner reads (its design's "Shared files and anchors", item 2).
         */
        fun taskOptions(model: String?, live: Boolean?): Map<String, String> = buildMap {
            model?.let { put("model", it) }
            live?.let { put("live", it.toString()) }
            // sub-project 5B
        }

        /** How Kestrel's Requests read [text]: the request's kind in lower case, such as other or stop, or none. */
        fun readOf(text: String, named: Boolean): String = Requests.read(text, named)?.javaClass?.simpleName?.lowercase() ?: "none"

        /** The kind and the outcome of command [number], as voice-state answers them; none and none for 0. */
        fun kindAndOutcome(graph: AppGraph, number: Int): Pair<String, String> {
            if (number <= 0) return "none" to "none"
            val state = graph.listening.state.value
            val recent = graph.listening.recentCommand(number)
            val runningNow = number == state.command && state.work == Work.RUNNING_COMMAND
            val kind = state.running?.takeIf { runningNow }?.javaClass?.simpleName ?: recent?.kind ?: "none"
            val outcome = when {
                runningNow -> "running"
                recent != null -> recent.outcome?.name?.lowercase() ?: "running"
                number in 1..state.command -> "unknown"
                else -> "none"
            }
            return kind to outcome
        }
```

with:

```kotlin
        if (intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_CLEAR, false)) {
            notices.forEach(manager::cancel)
            // Android takes them away a moment later, so the answer waits up to 2 s for that.
            withTimeoutOrNull(CHECK_WAIT_MILLIS) {
                while (manager.activeNotifications.any { it.id in notices }) delay(POLL_MILLIS)
            }
        }
        // The kinds come from the IDs, and for the stop notice from its fixed text, read on the phone (plan 4D, H6).
        val active = manager.activeNotifications.filter { it.id in notices }
        val kinds = noticeKinds(active.map { it.id to it.notification.extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_TEXT)?.toString() })
        return "notices=${active.size} kinds=$kinds"
    }

    /**
     * Posts (--ez on true) or takes away (--ez on false) Kestrel's own notification "Kestrel check", which the A71 checks
     * expand, collapse and dismiss by voice in place of the shell's notification, which no shell command can take away
     * (plan 4D, H6). Its text has two lines, so that One UI offers to expand it. Answers whether it is in the shade, after
     * waiting up to 2 s for Android to post or take it away.
     */
    private suspend fun checkNotice(intent: Intent): String {
        val manager = context.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java) ?: throw HookError("the notification service is missing")
        fun shown() = manager.activeNotifications.any { it.id == CHECK_ID }
        if (intent.hasExtra(EXTRA_ON)) {
            val on = intent.getBooleanExtra(EXTRA_ON, false)
            if (on) {
                if (context.checkSelfPermission(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                    throw HookError("Kestrel may not post notifications; allow them first")
                }
                val channel = NotificationChannel(CHECK_CHANNEL, CHECK_TITLE, NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT).apply { setSound(null, null) }
                manager.createNotificationChannel(channel)
                val notification = Notification.Builder(context, CHECK_CHANNEL)
                    .setSmallIcon(android.R.drawable.stat_notify_more)
                    .setContentTitle(CHECK_TITLE)
                    .setContentText(CHECK_TEXT)
                    .setStyle(Notification.BigTextStyle().bigText(CHECK_TEXT))
                    .build()
                manager.notify(CHECK_ID, notification)
            } else {
                manager.cancel(CHECK_ID)
                // So that the check leaves no channel of its own in Kestrel's notification settings.
                manager.deleteNotificationChannel(CHECK_CHANNEL)
            }
            withTimeoutOrNull(CHECK_WAIT_MILLIS) {
                while (shown() != on) delay(POLL_MILLIS)
            }
        }
        return "checkNotice=${if (shown()) "shown" else "gone"}"
    }
```


with:

```kotlin
        val COMMANDS = setOf(
            "VOICE_COUNTS", "VOICE_TIMES", "VOICE_ROUTE", "VOICE_SPOTTER", "VOICE_TASK", "VOICE_ECHO", "OWNER_OPTIONS", "NOTICES",
            "CHECK_NOTICE",
        )

        /**
         * The kinds of Kestrel's notices about problems, from their IDs and the stop notice's text, sorted and joined with
         * commas, or none: battery (it stopped because its battery use is restricted), stopped (another reason),
         * accessibility and settings (plan 4D, H6).
         */
        fun noticeKinds(notices: List<Pair<Int, String?>>): String = notices.mapNotNull { (id, text) ->
            when (id) {
                ProblemNotifications.STOPPED_ID -> if (text == StopReason.BATTERY_RESTRICTED.text) "battery" else "stopped"
                ProblemNotifications.ACCESSIBILITY_ID -> "accessibility"
                ProblemNotifications.SETTINGS_ID -> "settings"
                else -> null
            }
        }.distinct().sorted().joinToString(",").ifEmpty { "none" }

        // The notification "Kestrel check" of plan 4D's checks.
        private const val CHECK_ID = 7_190
        private const val CHECK_CHANNEL = "kestrel-check"
        private const val CHECK_TITLE = "Kestrel check"
        private const val CHECK_TEXT = "A notification of Kestrel's own checks.\nIt has a second line, so that it can expand."
        private const val CHECK_WAIT_MILLIS = 2_000L
        private const val POLL_MILLIS = 100L
```

```kotlin
    @Test
    fun aTaggedTranscriptAnswersWhatItsOwnCommandDidAndATagIsUsedOnce() {
        VoiceTests.assumeModels(Engine.VAD, Engine.KWS, Engine.ASR, Engine.TTS)
        VoiceTests.grantListeningPermissions()
        VoiceTests.useMicrophone(ClipMicrophone())

        ActivityScenario.launch(MainActivity::class.java).use {
            assertEquals("sent toggle", hook("VOICE", "event" to "toggle").text)
            assertTrue("The voice session did not open", VoiceTests.waitUntil(60_000) { listening.sessionOpen.value })
            val tag = "own-${System.nanoTime()}"

            assertEquals("sent heard tag=$tag", hook("VOICE", "event" to "heard", "text" to "Scroll down.", "tag" to tag).text)
            // A transcript right after it, as a stray utterance, does not take the tag's command.
            hook("VOICE", "event" to "heard", "text" to "Scroll up.")
            val ended = VoiceTests.waitUntil(10_000) { hook("VOICE_STATE", "tag" to tag).text.contains(" phase=ended ") }
            assertTrue("The tagged command did not end", ended)
            val own = hook("VOICE_STATE", "tag" to tag).text
            val line = Regex(
                "tag=$tag role=command command=\\d+ task=0 kind=Scroll outcome=(done|failed|ignored|cancelled) asking=false phase=ended " +
                    "detail=none said=\\S+ shown=\\S+ read=other latest=\\d+",
            )
            assertTrue(own, line.matches(own))
            assertEquals(
                HookReply(HookReply.FAILED, "error: the tag $tag is in use; give each transcript a new tag"),
                hook("VOICE", "event" to "heard", "text" to "Scroll up.", "tag" to tag),
            )
            assertEquals(HookReply(HookReply.FAILED, "error: no transcript has the tag never-sent"), hook("VOICE_STATE", "tag" to "never-sent"))
            assertTrue("Kestrel still speaks", VoiceTests.waitUntil(20_000) { !listening.state.value.speaking })

            // A tagged transcript that starts a task gets the task's number, and its extras wait for the task's runner.
            val taskTag = "task-${System.nanoTime()}"
            val sent = hook("VOICE", "event" to "heard", "text" to "Kestrel, open the settings.", "tag" to taskTag, "model" to "test-model", named = true)
            assertEquals("sent heard tag=$taskTag", sent.text)
            assertTrue("The tagged task did not start", VoiceTests.waitUntil(10_000) { hook("VOICE_STATE", "tag" to taskTag).text.contains(" role=task ") })
            val taskLine = hook("VOICE_STATE", "tag" to taskTag).text
            val task = Regex(" task=(\\d+) ").find(taskLine)?.groupValues?.get(1)?.toInt() ?: 0
            assertTrue(taskLine, task > 0 && taskLine.contains(" command=0 ") && taskLine.contains(" read=task "))
            assertEquals(mapOf("model" to "test-model"), listening.taskOptions?.invoke(task))
            assertEquals(emptyMap<String, String>(), listening.taskOptions?.invoke(task))
            assertTrue("Kestrel still speaks", VoiceTests.waitUntil(20_000) { !listening.state.value.speaking })

            assertEquals("sent off", hook("VOICE", "event" to "off").text)
            assertTrue("The voice session did not close", VoiceTests.waitUntil(30_000) { !listening.sessionOpen.value })
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun quietMakesTheClipMicrophoneKestrelsAndItDeliversSilenceWithNothingQueued() {
        val previous = listening.microphones
        try {
            assertEquals(
                HookReply(
                    Activity.RESULT_OK,
                    "quiet: Kestrel hears only the silent clip microphone from its next start, until voice-clip -Clear or the end of its process",
                ),
                hook("VOICE_CLIP", quiet = true),
            )
            assertSame(VoiceHooks.microphone, listening.microphones())
            assertTrue(hook("VOICE_STATE").text.endsWith(" clips=on"))
            // Kestrel is off, so the test records itself: frames come every 20 ms, and all of them are silent.
            val frames = AtomicInteger()
            val sound = AtomicBoolean(false)
            VoiceHooks.microphone.start(onFrame = { frame -> frames.incrementAndGet(); if (frame.any { it != 0f }) sound.set(true) }, onError = {})
            try {
                Thread.sleep(1_000)
            } finally {
                VoiceHooks.microphone.stop()
            }
            assertTrue("Only ${frames.get()} frames came", frames.get() >= 25)
            assertFalse("The quiet microphone delivered sound", sound.get())

            assertEquals(
                "dropped the waiting clips; Kestrel uses its own microphone again from its next start",
                hook("VOICE_CLIP", clear = true).text,
            )
            assertSame(previous, listening.microphones)
        } finally {
            listening.microphones = previous
            VoiceHooks.microphone.clear()
        }
    }
```

```kotlin
    /**
     * Runs a hook command as TestHookReceiver does, with string extras, the boolean extras `named` and `clear` and the
     * integer extra `number`.
     */
    private fun hook(
        command: String,
        vararg extras: Pair<String, String>,
        named: Boolean = false,
        clear: Boolean = false,
        number: Int? = null,
    ): HookReply {
        val intent = Intent(TestHooks.ACTION_PREFIX + command)
        for ((key, value) in extras) intent.putExtra(key, value)
        if (number != null) intent.putExtra("number", number)
        if (named) intent.putExtra("named", true)
        if (clear) intent.putExtra("clear", true)
```

```kotlin
    /**
     * Runs a hook command as TestHookReceiver does, with string extras, the boolean extras `named`, `clear` and `quiet` and
     * the integer extra `number`.
     */
    private fun hook(
        command: String,
        vararg extras: Pair<String, String>,
        named: Boolean = false,
        clear: Boolean = false,
        number: Int? = null,
        quiet: Boolean = false,
    ): HookReply {
        val intent = Intent(TestHooks.ACTION_PREFIX + command)
        for ((key, value) in extras) intent.putExtra(key, value)
        if (number != null) intent.putExtra("number", number)
        if (named) intent.putExtra("named", true)
        if (clear) intent.putExtra("clear", true)
        if (quiet) intent.putExtra("quiet", true)
```

### Task 3: Speech through the air: `voice-expect` (H2), the pause's end (H5) and the level (H7)

**Files:**
- Modify: `core/voice/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/voice/listen/ListeningPipeline.kt` (`VoiceEvent.Heard.closedAt`)
- Create: `core/voice/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/voice/listen/LevelMeter.kt`
- Modify: `core/voice/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/voice/listen/MicCapture.kt` (`routeReport`)
- Modify: `app/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/orchestrator/Rules.kt` (`Event.Heard.closedAt`), `app/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/listen/VoiceSession.kt`, `app/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/listen/Timeline.kt`, `app/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/listen/Listening.kt` (`transcripts`)
- Create: `app/src/debug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/Expectation.kt`, `app/src/debug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/ExpectHooks.kt`
- Modify: `app/src/debug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/TestHooks.kt`, `app/src/debug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/EvalHooks.kt`
- Modify: `scripts/hook.ps1`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-kestrel-design.md` (16.1)
- Test: `core/voice/src/test/kotlin/dev/kestrel/voice/listen/LevelMeterTest.kt` (new), `core/voice/src/test/kotlin/dev/kestrel/voice/listen/ListeningPipelineTest.kt`, `app/src/test/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/listen/TimelineTest.kt`, `app/src/testDebug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/ExpectationTest.kt` (new), `app/src/androidTest/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/voice/VoiceHookTest.kt`, `app/src/androidTest/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/voice/SessionHookTest.kt`, `app/src/androidTest/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/voice/EvalHookTest.kt`, `scripts/tests/Hook.Tests.ps1`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's `VoiceHooks.tags`, `VoiceHooks.tag(graph, tag, event)`, `VoiceHooks.refused(text, hardware)`, `VoiceHooks.YES_CALL_REFUSED`, `VoiceHooks.kindAndOutcome(graph, number)`, `voice-clip -Quiet`, `Listening.steps`; `WordErrors.count(said, heard)`, `Normalizer.words(text)`, `CommandParser.parse(text)`, `Commands.sameAction(expected, heard, labels)`, `BoostWordBudget.screenNames(snapshot)`, `ScreenSession.snapshots.latest()`.
- Produces: `VoiceEvent.Heard(text, named, seconds, millis, endedAt = 0L, closedAt = 0L)`; `Event.Heard(text, named, spokeAt = null, seconds = 0f, endedAt = 0L, closedAt = 0L)`; `Timeline.segmentClosed`, `Timeline.transcribed` and `fun transcript(closedAt: Long)`; `@Volatile internal var Listening.transcripts: ((Event.Heard) -> Boolean)?`; `class LevelMeter { fun add(frame: FloatArray); fun report(): String }` with `companion fun dbfs(meanSquare: Double): String`; `internal class Expectation(tag, expected, noRun, until)` with `words`, `match`, `stray`, `split`, `errors`, `waiting(now)`, `holds(now)` and `offer(heard, speechEnded, closed, now, judge)`; `internal class ExpectHooks(graph)` with the command `VOICE_EXPECT`; `hook.ps1`'s `voice-expect` and `-NoRun`. The answers are those of `plan-4d-interfaces.md`, section 4.

Hooks H2, H5 and H7 of the map. The air pass plays generated clips through the PC's speakers. `voice-expect -Text t -Tag k` makes the phone compare the next transcripts of Kestrel's voice session with `t`, with voice-eval's comparison, so that an air check reads the result of its own clip: the first transcript with at most half the expected words wrong matches and runs with the tag `k` (or, with `-NoRun`, is only resolved as voice-eval's `command=` does and never acts); every other transcript while the expectation waits is dropped and counted as a stray, and as a split part when its words are a run of the expected words. `Listening` gets one more seam for it, a check of each transcript of a voice session, which can drop it. H5: the pipeline marks when the end-of-speech pause ended (the endpointer let the utterance go), and `Timeline` when its text reached Kestrel, so that spec 5.3's transcription time comes from the phone alone. H7: `voice-route` and `voice-record -Result` answer the RMS level in dBFS of all samples and of the loudest 20 ms frame, so that the PC sets its own volume.

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.voice.listen

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

/** The microphone's level for the debug hooks (plan 4D, H7). */
class LevelMeterTest {
    @Test
    fun theLevelsAreTheRmsOfAllSamplesAndOfTheLoudestFrame() {
        val meter = LevelMeter()

        // A frame whose mean square is 0.25 (-6.0 dBFS), and a silent frame: 0.125 over both (-9.0 dBFS).
```

```kotlin
    @Test
    fun aTranscriptMarksTheEndOfItsPauseAndWhenItsTextCame() {
        timeline.transcript(closedAt = 9_700L)
        // A transcript without a known end of its pause, as from a pipeline without a clock, keeps the latest known one.
```

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.app.debug

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

/** One expectation of hook H2 (plan 4D): which transcript is the played clip's, and what the others count as. */
class ExpectationTest {
    @Test
    fun theFirstTranscriptWithAtMostHalfItsWordsWrongMatchesAndTheOthersAreStraysOrSplitParts() {
        val expectation = Expectation("t", "Scroll to the top.", noRun = false, until = 20_000L)

        assertEquals(4, expectation.words)
        assertFalse(expectation.offer("Scroll", speechEnded = 100, closed = 800, now = 1_200))
        assertFalse(expectation.offer("Hey, play some music.", speechEnded = 300, closed = 1_000, now = 1_500))
        assertTrue(expectation.waiting(1_600))
        assertTrue(expectation.offer("Scroll to top.", speechEnded = 900, closed = 1_600, now = 2_100))

        assertEquals(Expectation.Match(1, 900, 1_600, 2_100), expectation.match)
        assertEquals(1, expectation.errors)
        assertEquals(2, expectation.stray)
        assertEquals(1, expectation.split)
        // After the match, the next transcripts go to Kestrel as usual.
        assertFalse(expectation.waiting(2_200))
        assertFalse(expectation.holds(2_200))
    }

    @Test
    fun withoutRunningTheCorpusPassHoldsEveryTranscriptUntilTheEndAndANoMatchGivesTheClosestStray() {
        val corpus = Expectation("c", "Call Mom.", noRun = true, until = 5_000L)

        assertTrue(corpus.offer("Call mom", speechEnded = 100, closed = 200, now = 300) { "Call" to "1" })
        assertEquals(Expectation.Match(0, 100, 200, 300, kind = "Call", right = "1"), corpus.match)
        assertTrue(corpus.holds(4_999))
        assertFalse(corpus.holds(5_000))
        assertFalse(corpus.offer("Mom.", speechEnded = 400, closed = 500, now = 600))
        assertEquals(1, corpus.stray)

        val late = Expectation("l", "Open the settings.", noRun = false, until = 1_000L)
        assertFalse(late.offer("Hello there", speechEnded = 100, closed = 200, now = 300))
        assertFalse(late.offer("Open.", speechEnded = 400, closed = 500, now = 600))
        assertFalse(late.waiting(1_000))
        assertNull(late.match)
        assertEquals(2, late.errors)
        assertEquals(2, late.stray)
        assertEquals(1, late.split)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add `LevelMeter` and use it in `MicCapture`**

Create `core/voice/src/main/kotlin/dev/kestrel/voice/listen/LevelMeter.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.voice.listen

import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.log10

/**
 * The loudness of a recording's samples since the last [report], for the debug hooks voice-route and voice-record (plan
 * 4D, H7), so that the A71 checks set the PC's volume from what the phone hears: the RMS level of all samples and of the
 * loudest frame, in dBFS. Levels only, never audio. Any thread may call it.
 */
class LevelMeter {
    private var sumSquares = 0.0
    private var samples = 0L
    private var loudestFrame = 0.0

    /** Adds one frame of samples between -1 and 1. */
    @Synchronized
    fun add(frame: FloatArray) {
        if (frame.isEmpty()) return
        var sum = 0.0
        for (sample in frame) sum += sample.toDouble() * sample
        sumSquares += sum
        samples += frame.size
        loudestFrame = maxOf(loudestFrame, sum / frame.size)
    }

    /** "rms=<dBFS> frameRms=<dBFS>" since the last report, or none for both without a frame; then it starts again. */
    @Synchronized
    fun report(): String {
        val text = if (samples == 0L) "rms=none frameRms=none" else "rms=${dbfs(sumSquares / samples)} frameRms=${dbfs(loudestFrame)}"
        sumSquares = 0.0
        samples = 0L
        loudestFrame = 0.0
        return text
    }

    companion object {
        private const val FLOOR = -120.0

        /** The level of a mean square in dBFS, with one decimal, and -120.0 at the lowest, for silence. */
        fun dbfs(meanSquare: Double): String =
            String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.1f", if (meanSquare <= 0.0) FLOOR else maxOf(FLOOR, 10 * log10(meanSquare)))
    }
}
```

```kotlin
    /** When the latest transcribed utterance's end-of-speech pause ended, or 0 (plan 4D, H5). */

    fun transcript(closedAt: Long) {
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `Expectation`**

Create `app/src/debug/kotlin/dev/kestrel/app/debug/Expectation.kt`:

```kotlin
package dev.kestrel.app.debug

import dev.kestrel.commands.text.Normalizer
import dev.kestrel.commands.text.WordErrors
import java.util.Collections

/**
 * One expectation of hook H2 (plan 4D): the text [expected] that a clip played through the air should become, waited for
 * until [until] on the phone's clock. [offer] compares each transcript with voice-eval's comparison (WordErrors): the first
 * one with at most half the expected words wrong matches; every other one is a stray, and a stray whose words are a run
 * of the expected words, in order, is also a split part. [noRun]: the match is only resolved, never run, and every
 * transcript is held back until [until]. The texts stay in memory; it reports counts and times only.
 */
internal class Expectation(val tag: String, val expected: String, val noRun: Boolean, val until: Long) {
    /** The match: its word errors, its times on the phone's clock, and with [noRun] its command's kind and whether it is right. */
    data class Match(
        val errors: Int,
        val speechEnded: Long,
        val closed: Long,
        val transcribed: Long,
        val kind: String = "none",
        val right: String = "-",
    )

    val words: Int = WordErrors.count(expected, "").words

    var match: Match? = null
        private set
    var stray = 0
        private set
    var split = 0
        private set

    // The fewest errors of a stray, for an expectation without a match.
    private var best = words

    /** The errors of the match, or of the closest stray, or [words] when nothing came. */
    val errors: Int get() = match?.errors ?: best

    /** Whether it still waits for its match at [now]. */
    fun waiting(now: Long): Boolean = match == null && now < until

    /** Whether transcripts stay away from Kestrel at [now]: until the match, and with [noRun] until [until]. */
    fun holds(now: Long): Boolean = now < until && (match == null || noRun)

    /**
     * Compares [heard], whose speech ended at [speechEnded] and whose pause ended at [closed], and which came at [now]:
     * true when it is the match, false when it is a stray. [judge] gives the match's kind and whether it is right.
     */
    fun offer(
        heard: String,
        speechEnded: Long,
        closed: Long,
        now: Long,
        judge: (String) -> Pair<String, String> = { "none" to "-" },
    ): Boolean {
        val errors = WordErrors.count(expected, heard).errors
        if (match == null && words > 0 && errors * 2 <= words) {
            val (kind, right) = judge(heard)
            match = Match(errors, speechEnded, closed, now, kind, right)
            return true
        }
        stray++
        best = minOf(best, errors)
        if (isRun(Normalizer.words(heard), Normalizer.words(expected))) split++
        return false
    }

    private fun isRun(part: List<String>, whole: List<String>): Boolean =
        part.isNotEmpty() && part.size < whole.size && Collections.indexOfSubList(whole, part) >= 0
}
```
