```powershell
git add docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-kestrel-design.md README.md docs/a71-owner-guide.md
git commit -m "docs(sessions): describe the automatic A71 run, the sitting of 2026-11-20 and the owner's guide" -m "Co-authored-by: Copilot <223556219+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com>"
```

### Task 12: The A71 run and its results (the controller)

**Files:**
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-kestrel-design.md` (19.1, the bullet "The automatic A71 run (plan 4D)"), `README.md` (the table "The automatic A71 run")

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 6 to 11: the three session scripts, the owner's guide, `corpus\owner\a71-config.json`, the clips of Task 7, and `Unlock-TestPhone` for the lock check.
- Produces: the five results files of the A71 run in `corpus\owner`, and their numbers in spec 19.1 and the README.

The controller's task, not an implementer's: it runs on the A71 with the owner for his minutes, maybe days after Task 11, and it has its own commit. Task 11's documents and dry runs come first.

- [ ] **Step 1: The A71 run (the controller, with the owner for his minutes)**

The controller runs this step, not an implementer, when the owner is at this computer (process rules 9 and 13): he holds the emulator's lock, records the A71's run in STATUS.md, and no other agent uses the A71 or the emulator meanwhile. Before it: the A71 is on USB, unlocked, with no SIM card; `corpus\owner\a71-config.json` has `callNumber`, `testContacts`, `speakerTargetDb` and `pin` (the owner's guide); `corpus\generated\kokoro` and `piper` hold the clips of both lists (Task 7) and `corpus\generated\room-sound.wav` exists; this computer's speakers stand next to the A71. `<A71>` is the A71's serial from `adb devices`. No step passes `-AllowCallPress` (the scripts have no such switch any more): on the A71 Kestrel never presses Call while the owner is away, and the checks whose countdown would end with a press of Call run in the step `owner`, while the owner is there (AGENTS.md; process rules 10 and 13; the A71 has no SIM card, but it can still call emergency numbers). The order follows the owner's guide, so that his three items come within about 30 minutes:

0. With the owner, once before the long runs, because no script has unlocked One UI with the test PIN yet: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; . scripts\lib\Session.ps1; . scripts\lib\Oracle.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; Assert-TestDevice -Serial <A71>; Invoke-SettingsCommand -Serial <A71> -Command 'input keyevent KEYCODE_SLEEP' | Out-Null; Start-Sleep -Seconds 5; "locked: $(Test-KeyguardShowing @(Invoke-SettingsCommand -Serial <A71> -Command 'dumpsys activity activities'))"; "unlocked: $(Unlock-TestPhone -Serial <A71> -ConfigPath corpus\owner\a71-config.json)"`. Expected: `locked: True` and `unlocked: True`, and the A71 shows its home screen. With `unlocked: False` the script typed nothing, because Android did not say clearly that the PIN pad shows (One UI's view IDs of the PIN pad, or its keyguard line, differ from those of `Get-PinPadState`): the owner unlocks the A71 by hand, and the controller fixes `Get-PinPadState` before the runs. "the unlock failed" means that the PIN in the file did not unlock the A71: the owner corrects it before the runs. Then the same after a restart, as `listen-check.ps1 -Reboot` will find the A71, before its first unlock: `. scripts\lib\Kestrel.ps1; . scripts\lib\Session.ps1; . scripts\lib\Oracle.ps1; Use-KestrelToolchain; Assert-TestDevice -Serial <A71>; & (Get-Adb) -s <A71> reboot; & (Get-Adb) -s <A71> wait-for-device; do { Start-Sleep -Seconds 5 } until ("$(& (Get-Adb) -s <A71> shell getprop sys.boot_completed)".Trim() -eq '1'); Start-Sleep -Seconds 20; "unlocked: $(Unlock-TestPhone -Serial <A71> -ConfigPath corpus\owner\a71-config.json)"`. Expected: `unlocked: True` within about 2 minutes. With `unlocked: False` the owner unlocks it by hand, and the controller fixes `Get-PinPadState` before the runs, because a71-session's step `listen-check` restarts the A71 without him.
1. Without the owner: `scripts\a71-commands.ps1 -Serial <A71> -Step install`, `scripts\a71-session.ps1 -Serial <A71> -Step install` and `scripts\a71-providers.ps1 -Serial <A71> -EmulatorSerial emulator-5554 -LockHeld -Step install`. Expected: each ends with exit code 0, and its results file says that Kestrel (and, for the first, the fixture app) was installed.
2. With the owner: `scripts\a71-commands.ps1 -Serial <A71> -Step owner`. Expected: four `owner` lines, `call Ana Test, then the owner's finger on the clock [text]: … check=pass|fail (cancelled, with "Cancelled" on the bar, and no call)` and the three countdowns that `cancel`, `stop` and a tap end, the message that his item is done, and exit code 0.
3. With the owner: `scripts\a71-session.ps1 -Serial <A71> -Step earbuds`. Expected: the `earbuds` lines of the route, the music's route, "Kestrel" next to the earbuds and after Bluetooth went off, and exit code 0.
4. With the owner for its first 5 minutes: `scripts\a71-providers.ps1 -Serial <A71> -EmulatorSerial emulator-5554 -LockHeld -Step emulator-signin,a71-signin,emulator-live,a71-tests,a71-live,a71-restart,end`. Expected: `emulator-signin` and `a71-signin` say that the default profile is a Copilot profile, with "Kestrel's page came back by itself: yes|no" on the A71; then the live cases, the device tests, the restart and `end: … the A71 keeps its Copilot profile, as the owner decided`; exit code 0.
5. Without the owner: `scripts\a71-commands.ps1 -Serial <A71> -Step screen,swipes,notifications,global,profiles,radios,sound,text,calls,timing,air,corpus` and then `scripts\a71-session.ps1 -Serial <A71> -Unattended -Step voice-tests,failures,listen-check,battery-rules,activation,assistant,spotter,voice,memory,battery,failures-late`. Expected: both end with exit code 0 and no warning of a restore; the first's results end with `session: text: <n> of <m> checks passed`, `session: kokoro: …` and `session: piper: …`; the step `calls` says `call the confirmed number: skipped: on the A71 Kestrel never presses Call on purpose…` and that the countdowns ran in the step `owner`.

If a script stops early, its results say why; the controller decides whether to run its remaining steps again with `-Step`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the run's results into the spec and the README**

Fill the spec's bullet "The automatic A71 run (plan 4D)" and the README's table "The automatic A71 run" from the five results files, in the README's form below, without any word that Kestrel heard. In the spec, the bullet's last sentence "Their dry runs passed on kestrel-api37; the run on the A71 follows." becomes sentences of this form, with the run's own numbers and findings:

```text
On <date>, on the A71: the text pass passed <n> of <m> checks, the Kokoro clips <n> of <m> and the Piper clips <n> of <m>; the corpus pass matched <n> of 160 Kokoro clips and <n> of 160 Piper clips, with <x> % and <y> % of the commands right; the transcription time through the air had a median of <n> ms and a 90th percentile of <n> ms (target at most 4 s); the owner's finger <did|did not> cancel the countdown (spike S3), and "cancel", "stop" and the tap cancelled <n> of 3 countdowns; <the session's and the providers' main results, one sentence each>; the failed checks were <the checks' names>.
```

and each README row's two cells take the script's own counts and its notable findings, such as `<n> of <m> (text), <n> of <m> (Kokoro), <n> of <m> (Piper)` and `the finger <did|did not> cancel the countdown`. Then commit:

```powershell
git add docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-kestrel-design.md README.md
git commit -m "docs(sessions): record the automatic A71 run's results" -m "Co-authored-by: Copilot <223556219+Copilot@users.noreply.github.com>"
```

## Self-review

**Map coverage.** `a71-commands.ps1` (Task 8): C01 to C03 in `install`; C04 and C106 by the runner's refusal of "yes, call" and its "no" to every question that a check did not plan; C05 to C24 in `screen` (C21 through `screen-has -Template`, text pass only); C25 to C33 in `swipes`; C34 to C39 in `notifications` with the hook `check-notice`; C40 to C57 in `global`, with C48 and C49 there through the test PIN; C58 and C59 in `profiles`; C60 to C63 in `radios` with `detail`; C64 to C87 in `sound`, with the sound mode put back by Kestrel's own command where the shell cannot; C88 to C104 in `text`, in the fixture app's fields (not Samsung Notes, below); C105 to C117 in `calls`, with C115's real finger in `owner`; on the A71 the countdowns of C113 and C114 and an injected tap during a countdown run in `owner` too, while the owner is there, and the call to the confirmed number (C108) only in the dry run, because on the A71 Kestrel never presses Call on purpose (process rules 10 and 13); C118 and C119 in `timing`; C120 at the start of `air`; C121 in `corpus`. `a71-session.ps1` (Task 9): S01 to S43, S13 through `listen-check.ps1`'s test PIN and S23 as the owner's one item; S24 through Kestrel's own route. `a71-providers.ps1` (Task 10): P01 to P15, P08 answered by the redirect case, P14 by the owner's decision to keep the A71's profile. Hooks: H1 to H7 and `clipboard` are called only by the names and answers of `plan-4d-interfaces.md`.

**Placeholder scan.** Every code step holds the whole file or the exact old and new text. The only forms with blanks are Task 12 Step 2's sentences for the A71 run's numbers, which exist only after the run.

**Type consistency.** Task 6's names are used unchanged in Tasks 7 to 10: `Invoke-AutoCheck` with `-Answer`, `-Respond`, `-RespondAt`, `-RespondDelay`, `-Send`, `-Clip`, `-Play`, `-Wait` and `-WaitSeconds`; `Set-SpeakerLevel`, `Invoke-Clip`, `Start-ClipLoop`, `Stop-ClipLoop`, `Get-PhoneClock`, `Get-ClipSpeechEnd`; `Get-ResumedPackage`, `Get-HomePackage`, `Get-Wakefulness`, `Test-KeyguardShowing`, `Test-InputShown`, `Get-PlaybackState`, `Test-StreamMuted`, `Get-FocusedWindow`, `Get-TextHash`, `Test-HashedText`; `Read-A71Config`, `Send-TestPin`, `Unlock-TestPhone`. The hooks' parameters are those of the interfaces file: `-Tag`, `-Quiet`, `-NoRun`, `-Package`, `-ViewId`, `-Label`, `-Contains`, `-ClassName`, `-State`, `-Template`, `-Expected`, `-On`, `-Save`, `-Restore`. The check corpus's ids `k001` to `k023` are the ones that Tasks 8 and 9 play.

**Device safety.** Every device step of Tasks 7 to 11 runs on kestrel-api37 with the emulator check in the same command; Task 12, which the controller runs, is the A71 run. On the A71 Kestrel never presses Call while the owner is away, and never on purpose: `a71-commands.ps1` has no `-AllowCallPress` any more, the call to the confirmed number runs only in a dry run, the countdowns run only in the step `owner` while he is there, and the runner cancels every countdown that a check did not plan (`Invoke-AutoCheck`). The sign-ins tap and wait only for labels of the package `dev.kestrel`, never Chrome's, and put back the A71's and this computer's clipboards; no A71 script runs the test package `dev.kestrel.app.debug`. No script sends "yes, call", and every one refuses other devices; only `Send-TestPin` reads the key `pin`, only on the A71 and right before it types the PIN on the lock screen through its own call of adb, never through `Invoke-SettingsCommand`, whose errors repeat the command; it clears the PIN after use, no script holds the PIN in a variable of its own, `Read-A71Config` leaves the key out, and nothing writes the PIN; `Session.Tests.ps1` checks that `Read-A71Config`'s result has no `pin`, and with a fake adb that fails that no output, warning or error holds the PIN.

**Sub-project 5's requests.** `ConvertFrom-TaggedState` reads `voice-state -Tag`'s field `task` (writer A's H1, `role=task`), and the fake answers of Task 6's tests carry `task=0`; `Invoke-AutoCheck -Wait -WaitSeconds` (at most 600) replaces the wait for the command's end with the caller's script block, tested with a fake `agent-task -Result -Tag`; and `Read-A71Config` is the reader without the key `pin`.
